High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

- Fri, 20 Nov 2026 06:42:00 -0300 — Zumbi é farol para luta em defesa da democracia, diz pesquisador
  Mais de 300 anos atrás, tropas coloniais juntavam forças para atacar e destruir definitivamente o Quilombo dos Palmares . Por cerca de um século, esse território dava proteção e garantia à liberdade de centenas de negros que haviam conseguido escapar da escravidão. Era a segunda metade do século 17, o Brasil ainda era parte do Império Português e não havia qualquer movimento das autoridades para que a escravidão fosse abolida no país. - Thu, 19 Nov 2026 20:34:00 -0300 — Prêmio Brasil Olímpico 2026 revela finalistas a melhor atleta do ano
  O Comitê Olímpico Brasileiro (COB) anunciou nesta quinta-feira (19) os finalistas à categoria de melhor atleta do ano do Prêmio Brasil Olímpico 2026. No gênero feminino, concorrem Maria Clara Pacheco (taekwondo), Rebeca Andrade (ginástica artística) e a seleção de ginástica rítmica. Já no masculino a corrida pelo troféu reúne Alison dos Santos (atletismo), Hugo Calderano (tênis de mesa) e Lucas Pinheiro Braathen (esqui alpino). Os vencedores serão conhecidos durante cerimônia de gala no dia 2 de dezembro, a partir das 18h45 (horário de Brasília), na Cidade das Artes, no Rio de Janeiro. Notícias relacionadas: Alison dos Santos conquista o título da Liga Diamante pela 3ª vez. Rebeca Andrade conquista o tri no Mundial de Ginástica na Holanda .   Cleaning up space: OHB awarded EUR 102 million contract by ESA for Europe’s first active space debris removal mission - ohb.de Skip to content Cleaning up space: OHB awarded EUR 102 million contract by ESA for Europe’s first active space debris removal mission Bremen, November 17, 2026 Low Earth Orbit (LEO) is becoming increasingly congested as the amount of space debris continues to grow. Thousands of defunct satellites and millions of debris fragments orbit Earth, increasing the risk of collisions. With the ClearSpace-1 mission (CS-1), Europe aims to demonstrate for the first time how a satellite can be captured and safely removed from orbit in a controlled manner. For this pioneering mission, the European Space Agency (ESA) has selected OHB System AG as prime contractor. Back ‘Trust Me, It’s Gonna Take Off’: Inside Electron’s First 100 Launches Share this Rocket Lab team members at Launch Complex 1 in Māhia, New Zealand, celebrate Electron’s 100th launch. By Matt McKinney It started with about 10 people, a carbon-fiber rocket that skeptics said would never work, and a sheep farm at the end of a windswept New Zealand peninsula. On Nov. 13, 2026, Electron flew for the 100 th time , reaching the mark faster than any other commercially developed rocket in history. Electron, which took roughly four years to develop, now rolls off the factory floor every eight days. Even by today's fast-moving aerospace standards, few small launch vehicles have reached even double-digi

What would happen if an asteroid the size of the Great Pyramid of Giza was on a collision course with Earth? Its impact could destroy entire cities, or generate a devastating tsunami if it struck the ocean. Would humanity be prepared to respond to such a threat? That is precisely what the European Space Agency's Hera mission aims to help determine. On September 26 th 2022, NASA’s Double Asteroid Redirection Test (DART) mission carried out humanity’s first-ever asteroid deflection experiment by deliberately crashing its 580 kg spacecraft into Dimorphos, a moonlet measuring 151 meters in diameter, at a speed of 6.1 km/s.   We’ve managed to take high-resolution orbital images of many worlds across our solar system, and many of them with an atmosphere have one feature in common - sand dunes. Scientists have been using the size and orientation of these dunes for decades to act like a weather vane, figuring out which way the wind blows on these other worlds. Unfortunately, weather vanes aren’t good at telling the full windy story - they can’t tell you how fast the wind is going. - Wed, 18 Nov 2026 20:05:05 +0000 — The Simons Observatory is Online and Surveying the Cosmic Microwave Background
  The Simons Observatory is online and surveying the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) in greater detail than ever before. - Wed, 18 Nov 2026 19:11:15 +0000 — Are Uranus and Neptune Secretly "Rock Giants"? A Noble Gas Might Hold the Answer
  For decades, astronomers have divided the planets in the outer solar system into two distinct camps. Jupiter and Saturn were the “gas giants” made up primarily of hydrogen and helium. Uranus and Neptune were the “ice giants”, and were much smaller but were packed with heavier elements. A new paper from Olivier Mousis of the Southwest Research Institute and his co-authors, available in The Planetary Science Journal, explores whether we can use an isotope of a noble gas - argon-40 - to determine whether there are hidden mountains of rock under the ice giant’s thick atmospheres.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/are-uranus-and-neptune-secretly-rock-giants-a-noble-gas-might-hold-the-answer

  Astronomers used the Very Large Baseline Array of radio telescopes to measure a pair of active red dwarfs with extreme precision. - Wed, 18 Nov 2026 17:23:14 +0000 — Fermi’s 14-Year All-Sky Map Bridges Astronomy’s Elusive "MeV Gap"
  Gamma rays, the most high-energy type of electromagnetic radiation in the universe, have always had a blind spot. We’ve always had trouble measuring them in the “mega-electron volt” (MeV) energy range, which astronomers have taken to calling the “MeV gap”. But a new paper from an international group of scientists working with the Fermi Large Area Telescope (LAT) Collaboration recently published a paper in The Astrophysical Journal Supplement Series that takes a stab at closing that gap by identifying 450 cosmic sources in the 20-200 MeV range, providing the clearest view of low-energy gamma rays ever.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/fermis-14-year-all-sky-map-bridges-astronomys-elusive-mev-gap
- Wed, 18 Nov 2026 15:01:09 +0000 — Microscopic Space Dust Reveals A Hidden Reservoir Of "Heavy Water" Beyond Neptune
  Astronomers can find valuable information in even the tiniest objects. That was certainly the case recently when twelve dust grains collected by a high-altitude aircraft helped solve a mystery about the birth of our Solar System. Or at least that is the argument in a new paper published in Science Advances by researchers at NASA’s Johnson Space Center and UCLA. According to the paper, the region beyond Neptune once had a massive isolated reservoir of “heavy water” that never mixed in with the inner planets.

- undated — TraCSS Registration for Spacecraft Operators
  TraCSS Registration for Spacecraft Operators &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content TraCSS Registration for Spacecraft Operators TraCSS encourages interested spacecraft operators to sign-up for the&nbsp; Traffic Coordination System for Space &nbsp;(TraCSS). Operators are encouraged to review the&nbsp; TraCSS User Agreement &amp; Data Policy &nbsp;prior to signing up. Note that at this&nbsp;time, TraCSS accounts are only available to organizations that own and/or operate spacecraft in orbit, and National Government users . Other entities (researchers, commercial&nbsp;entities, etc.) interested in receiving updates on the TraCSS program are encouraged to sign-up for the Office of Space Commerce “stakeholder engagement” contact lists,&nbsp; here . Sign-Up Process: As of May 2026, the Traffic Coordination System for Space is accepting satellite owner/operator and Nation
  https://space.commerce.gov/traffic-coordination-system-for-space-tracss/tracss-registration-for-spacecraft-operators/
- undated — Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification
  Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background: This test set was developed for the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and is designed to help space situational awareness (SSA) service providers test their conjunction assessment (CA) algorithms. The dataset comes with an accompanying &#8220;answer key&#8221; that lists the conjunctions in the dataset &#8211; providing a voluntary tool for self-assessment. DISCLAIMER : While this ephemeris dataset was crafted to include stressing edge cases in addition to an operational catalog, it is not completely comprehensive (as there are nearly infinite combinations of orbits and conjunction geometries possible). This data was not evaluated (nor is it intended) for use in live operations or as a tool for formal system certification or validation . These data are ava
  https://space.commerce.gov/dataset-for-conjunction-assessment-verification/
- undated — TraCSS-EU SST Joint Studies & Service Comparison
  TraCSS-EU SST Joint Studies &amp; Service Comparison &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background Space debris and collision avoidance are challenges for all spacecraft, regardless of their nation of registry. As laid out in the Office of Space Commerce’s&nbsp; Vision for Global SSA Coordination , international collaboration is key to enhancing data consistency, coverage, and transparency. Both the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and the European Union Space Surveillance and Tracking (EU SST), in partnership with the commercial space situational awareness (SSA) industry, will offer free space SSA services to spacecraft operators worldwide to prevent in-orbit collisions. A series of studies, conducted by personnel from both programs, highlight similarities and differences between the two systems. These studies represent a step toward greater trans

- undated — European Hardware Takes Center Stage at Space Tech Expo
  European Hardware Takes Center Stage at Space Tech Expo Skip to content Europe is a region chock full of builders. At least, that’s the vibe we got this week watching the news wire from Space Tech Expo in Bremen, Germany. Like all space conferences, the Expo coincided with a firehose of announcements, but one theme stood out: Europe is building a metric ship-load of hardware. Production lineup: For well over a year, Payload Europe has covered the growth in manufacturing capacity coming from the region’s satellite manufacturers, and components suppliers. In all, Europe is on the verge of being able to produce high-hundreds of satellites annually, and demand from programs like IRIS 2 will require at least as much.&nbsp; Space Tech Expo proved that Europe’s manufacturing boom is still on an upswing—and it extends far beyond the production of satellite buses and subcomponents.   https://payloadspace.com/european-hardware-takes-center-stage-at-space-tech-expo/
- undated — Sophia Space Tests Orbital-Compute Software on Kepler Constellation
  Sophia Space Tests Orbital-Compute Software on Kepler Constellation Skip to content Sophia Space has deployed its Sophia Orbital Operating System (SOOS) across multiple Kepler Communications optical-data-relay sats. The test, announced today, marks the first in-space milestone toward Sophia’s goal of scalable orbital-compute infrastructure, with SOOS as the base software layer for future architectures. As more data flows to and from—as well as between—satellites, operators are looking to process, manage, and deliver that data as efficiently as possible. Sophia is pitching SOOS as a way to do that across a distributed network in orbit. “We believe [the test] is the first time there will be a distributed network computer in orbit,” Sophia Space CEO and cofounder Rob DeMillo told Payload as the deployment was ongoing.&nbsp; Under the hood: SOOS is the software layer for Sophia’s future TILE

- undated — Voyager 1 Reaches New Milestone: One Light-Day from Earth
  Voyager 1 Reaches New Milestone: One Light-Day from Earth &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com NASA&#8217;s Voyager 1 spacecraft achieved another milestone this morning. Launched in 1977, it became the first human object to pass into interstellar space in August 2012 and is so far from Earth now that it takes one entire day for a radio signal to get there &#8212; one light-day. It also takes one light-day for Voyager 1 to send back the data it is still acquiring after all these decades. Voyager 1 passed the one light-day threshold at 5:16:07 am ET this morning at a distance of 16.094 billion miles (25.902 billion kilometers). That means it takes two days to communicate with Voyager &#8212; one day for the signal to get there and another day for the answer to come back. The spacecraft sent back stunning images of Jupiter in 1979 and Saturn in 1980. Since then it&#8217;s been on a quest to find w
  https://spacepolicyonline.com/news/voyager-1-reaches-new-milestone-one-light-day-from-earth/

  Insufficient Fuel Flow May Have Doomed Vulcan &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com The United Launch Alliance said today that insufficient fuel flow from the launch vehicle to the BE-4 engines may have led to the failure of Vulcan Leo 1 on Sunday. The fifth flight of ULA&#8217;s new Vulcan rocket failed about 10 minutes after liftoff on November 15, the company&#8217;s first total failure in 20 years of operation. By yesterday they had traced the problem to &#8220;low booster performance,&#8221; referring to the rocket&#8217;s first stage. The first stage is powered by two BE-4 liquid methane/liquid oxygen (methalox) engines built by Blue Origin, which uses them for its own New Glenn rocket. In an update today, ULA says preliminary indications are that it was not the engines themselves, but &#8220;inadequate fuel supply from the vehicle&#8221; to the engines. - undated — ICEYE unveils Gen5 satellite, makes €2-billion bid for Avio
  ICEYE unveils Gen5 satellite, makes €2-billion bid for Avio Close Search for: Search Close Skip to content An ICEYE Gen5 synthetic aperture radar satellite. Credit: ICEYE. ICEYE, the Finnish company whose synthetic aperture radar (SAR) satellites have become an important part of Earth observation for both commercial and governmental clients, has made a pair of announcements this week that could expand its business and have an impact on European space sovereignty. Its first announcement , made on Monday, was the official unveiling of its Gen5 Earth observation SAR satellites. The second (and somewhat more surprising) announcement on Tuesday was of its intention to acquire Italian launcher company Avio, through ICEYE’s Italian subsidiary Iris 2 S.p.A. The acquisition, ICEYE said, would be a boost for European space sovereignty, particularly for “sovereign space and defense capabilities.” S
  https://spaceq.ca/iceye-gen5-avio-bid/
- undated — GHGSat DATA.AIR Gen2 wins EPA approval for leak screening
  GHGSat DATA.AIR Gen2 wins EPA approval for leak screening Close Search for: Search Close Skip to content GHGSat announced Thursday that the U.S. Environmental Protection Agency (EPA) has approved DATA.AIR Gen2, its second-generation airborne methane sensor, as a way for oil and gas operators to meet federal leak-inspection rules, a commercial boost for its airborne monitoring business. The approval lets operators hire GHGSat to fly over their sites in place of sending ground crews to check for fugitive emissions, the methane that escapes from valves, connectors and other equipment. It applies under Subpart OOOOb, the EPA&#8217;s 2024 methane standard for new and modified oil and gas facilities, at the rule&#8217;s tier for leaks of 10 kilograms of methane an hour.   Dominion Dynamics, Telesat to build Arctic antennas for Lightspeed Close Search for: Search Close Skip to content Telesat Lightspeed Constellation illustration. According to the joint release , Ottawa-based Dominion Dynamics will lead the work as prime contractor, with Telesat LEO, a subsidiary of Telesat Corporation , as its subcontractor. The $2.2 million funds the first phase of development and includes a contribution to Dominion from the National Research Council of Canada&#8217;s Industrial Research Assistance Program, a federal program that helps companies pay for research and development. - undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht

#### ESA top news (government, en, feed)

- Thu, 19 Nov 2026 11:10:00 +0100 — Watch live: Hera’s first images of post-impact asteroid Dimorphos
  A little more than four years ago, humans moved an asteroid, as NASA’s DART spacecraft impacted a body called Dimorphos. Now the European Space Agency’s Hera spacecraft has arrived at that same asteroid to find out what happened next. Join the Hera team on the evening of Wednesday 25 November as they share the first close-up images of this historic transformed space object.
  https://www.esa.int/articles/about-us/directorates/operations/space-safety/hera/watch-live-hera-s-first-images-of-post-impact-asteroid-dimorphos
- Thu, 19 Nov 2026 00:00:00 +0100 — Seven-spacecraft observatory to study plasma universe
  ESA Member States have selected Plasma Observatory as the agency’s next medium-class mission. Its seven coordinated spacecraft will track how the Sun’s energy moves through Earth’s magnetic shield, across different scales of distance and time.
  https://www.esa.int/articles/about-us/directorates/science/seven-spacecraft-observatory-to-study-plasma-universe
- Wed, 18 Nov 2026 12:00:00 +0100 — First thermal images from Sentinel-3C radiometer
  These are the first images showing land- and sea-surface temperatures from the third satellite of the Copernicus Sentinel-3 mission, Sentinel-3C.
  https://www.esa.int/articles/about-us/directorates/earth-observation-programmes/copernicus/sentinel-3/first-thermal-images-from-sentinel-3c-radiometer
- Wed, 18 Nov 2026 11:00:00 +0100 — In the shadow of volcanic giants
  Even Mars’s more modest volcanoes demand attention. This month’s snapshot from ESA’s Mars Express spotlights Tharsis Tholus, a fascinating peak with an eventful past.
  https://www.esa.int/articles/about-us/directorates/science/mars-express/in-the-shadow-of-volcanic-giants
- Tue, 17 Nov 2026 13:03:28 +0100 — Prodex at 40: advancing European space science
  For forty years, Prodex, the European Space Agency’s Programme for the Development of Scientific Experiments, has turned the scientific ambitions of its Participating States into instruments and experiments flown in space.
  https://www.esa.int/articles/prodex-at-40-advancing-european-space-science
- Tue, 17 Nov 2026 09:59:44 +0100 — Hera surveying asteroids with superhuman vision
  The European Space Agency’s Hera mission for planetary defence surveys its target asteroids with a combination of instruments, peering beyond the limits of the human eye. The larger Didymos asteroid – dominated by its dramatic equatorial ridge – is seen here from just 28.5 km away, variously in visible light, multispectral and thermal infrared vision.
  https://www.esa.int/articles/about-us/directorates/operations/space-safety/hera/hera-surveying-asteroids-with-superhuman-vision

- undated — Chi mangia patate (lunari) non muore mai
  Chi mangia patate (lunari) non muore mai &#8211; MEDIA INAF Una delle principali sfide dell’esplorazione spaziale dei prossimi decenni è stabilire una presenza umana stabile e duratura sul nostro satellite naturale, un obiettivo a cui punta il programma Artemis della Nasa, che mira anche a gettare le basi per la futura esplorazione di Marte. Affinché ciò sia possibile, sarà tuttavia necessario garantire agli astronauti un approvvigionamento alimentare sostenibile. La permanenza prolungata sulla Luna dipenderà infatti anche dalla possibilità di produrre cibo direttamente sul posto, riducendo la necessità di costose missioni di rifornimento dalla Terra. Patate ottenute negli esperimenti condotti da David Handy e colleghi in un terreno simulante la regolite lunare. #### StartMag (press, it, text-reader)

- undated — Cosa prevede il nuovo Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale
  Nel corso della seduta del Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), il governo Meloni ha approvato Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale. Tutti i dettagli Palazzo Chigi accelera su autonomia strategica e space economy. Ieri il governo Meloni ha approvato il nuovo Documento Strategico di Politica Spaziale Nazionale (Dspsn), predisposto dall’Agenzia Spaziale Italiana (Asi) in coordinamento con il Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), in attuazione degli Indirizzi del Governo in materia spaziale e aerospaziale. In Italia, il coordinamento politico dello spazio è gestito infatti dalla Presidenza del Consiglio dei ministri, con il Presidente del Consiglio che presiede il Comint. Il piano appena approvato definirà le priorità italiane nello spazio per il prossimo decennio. In particolare, il documento individua quattro assi strategici – ricerca e benefici per la società, competitività industriale, governance e cooperazione internazionale – e punta a rafforzare l’autonomia strategica del Paese, la sicurezza delle infrastrutture spaziali e la leadership industriale italian
  https://www.startmag.it/spazio-e-difesa/cosa-prevede-il-nuovo-documento-strategico-di-politica-spaziale-nazionale/

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

- undated — pdf
  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm

Met de Innovatie Impact Challenge (ICC) Ruimtevaart voor het veiligheidsdomein investeert Nederland in vier innovatieve technologieën die een veilig en duurzaam gebruik van de ruimte bevorderen. - undated — tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie in 2025
  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nederland-verhoogt-inzet-tijdens-esa-ministersconferentie-in-bremen/
- undated — 18 november 2026 20 jaar ESERO Onderwijs: ruimtevaart als vonk voor toekomstig talent Lees meer
  20 jaar ESERO Onderwijs: ruimtevaart als vonk voor toekomstig talent - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Zelf een minisatelliet bouwen, trainen als een astronaut of de satelliet van de toekomst ontwerpen. Lessen en activiteiten van ESERO Onderwijs brengen al twintig jaar ruimtevaart in de klas. Gisteren vierde het programma zijn jubileum bij ESTEC in Noordwijk. ESERO onderwijs begon met een pilot in 2006. Na een succesvol scholenexperiment tijdens de eerste ruimtemissie van André Kuipers – bijna 80.000 leerlingen deden mee aan seeds in space – sloegen ESA en de Nederlandse overheid de handen ineen om werk te maken van ruimtevaarteducatie. Ruimtevaart kan scholieren inspireren en op het pad zetten van een carrière in wetenschap of techniek, was ook de overtuiging van het Ministerie van Onderwijs, Cultuur en Wetenschap. &nbsp; Twintig jaar later is ESERO onderwijs uitgegroeid

- undated — Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick
  Vertrag unterzeichnet: VBS erhält Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick Der Schweizer «Blindflug» ist bald zu Ende. Der Bund kann künftig Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich beziehen. Ein entsprechender Rahmenvertrag wurde unterzeichnet. Publiziert: 04.09.2026 um 04:25 Uhr | Aktualisiert: 29.06.2023 um 11:56 Uhr Teilen Kommentieren 1/5 Die Schweiz kann künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO beziehen (Symbolbild). Foto: AP Verteidigungsministerin Viola Amherd (61) macht einen weiteren Schritt auf das westliche Verteidigungsbündnis Nato zu. Soeben haben Frankreich und die Schweiz einen Rahmenvertrag unterzeichnet, der es dem Bund ermöglicht, künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO «Composante Spatiale Optique» zu beziehen. Selber hat die Schweiz keine Satelliten im All. Desha
  https://www.blick.ch/politik/vertrag-unterzeichnet-vbs-erhaelt-satellitenbilder-aus-frankreich/lv5p0cg

Diese sollen Unternehmen, Universitäten und Forschungseinrichtungen dazu motivieren, die Luftfahrtstrategie der Bundesregierung zu unterstützen. Der Projektträger Luftfahrtforschung tritt in diesem Zusammenhang als Verwaltungshelfer auf, der die Ausschreibungen bündelt und koordiniert. Forschende, die einmal in diesem Bereich gearbeitet haben, bestätigen immer wieder: Die Luftfahrt ist sehr speziell. Publicité » --> La création du Bureau des affaires spatiales monégasques vient renforcer l&rsquo;attrait de la Principauté pour ce secteur. Monaco gagne du terrain dans un domaine en pleine expansion : l’activité aérospatiale privée. Le 12 octobre dernier, le Gouvernement Princier a annoncé la création du Bureau des affaires spatiales, conformément à la volonté du Prince Albert II de diversifier l’économie et de renforcer l’attractivité de la Principauté. L’enjeu est triple : accompagner les entreprises du domaine spatial déjà implantées sur le territoire monégasque, créer une base attractive pour celles qui veulent s’installer en Principauté et représenter Monaco à l’inte

- undated — Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space
  Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu It has become a truism that Europe’s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. In contrast to continent-wide civilian programmes such as Galileo or Copernicus, military space assets remain scattered across the armed forces and governments of individual states. As a result, just four countries — France, Germany, Italy, and Spain — operate

  The Baltic as a ‘battlespace’: When diplomacy and Special Operations Forces work together [OPINION] Menu Following Russia’s full-scale invasion of Ukraine in 2022, Finland and Sweden decided to join the North Atlantic Alliance. For analysts and planners, however, it remains a maritime area riddled with gaps — in legislation, procedures and the architecture for sharing information.   Problemy techniczne sondy Hayabusa2. JAXA zmienia plany lotu Menu Modyfikacja planów została ogłoszona przez japońską agencję kosmiczną JAXA 17 listopada br. Decyzję podjęto po wykryciu awarii ostatniego sprawnego silnika jonowego sondy oraz oznak postępującego zużycia jej wyposażenia. Z przekazanych informacji wynika, że Hayabusa2 dysponuje czterema silnikami jonowymi, które wykorzystywane są do korekt trajektorii i stopniowego zwiększania prędkości sondy. W połowie sierpnia br. doszło do awarii ostatniego sprawnego silnika tego typu, co ograniczyło możliwości dalszego manewrowania. W trakcie wieloletniej podróży wykryto również oznaki erozji powierzchni sondy . 小惑星探査機「はやぶさ２」はイオンエンジンなどの不調により着実に遂行できる計画に変更することになり、新たに「2015 BM510」のフライバイ探査を行うこととしました。 🎥Activity Report mini🎥 #JAXA #はや２ #フライバイ https://t.co/INAcPpyoHw &mdash; JAXA（宇宙航空研究開発機構） (@JAXA_jp) November 19, 2026 Japońska misja na Księży

Ministrstvo za gospodarstvo, delo in šport ter Trgovinska zbornica Slovenije (TZS) zato podpirata uveljavitev razvojnega zakona, ki za izbrana osnovna živila trajno znižuje DDV z 9,5 na 5 odstotkov. Ministrstvi želita podjetja dejavneje vključiti v raziskave in razvoj, od opredelitve razvojnih izzivov do preizkušanja rešitev in njihovega uvajanja na

- undated — Sodelovanje z Evropsko vesoljsko agencijo
  Sodelovanje z Evropsko vesoljsko agencijo | GOV.SI Domov Področja Podjetništvo in gospodarstvo Inovativnost in tehnološki razvoj Sodelovanje z Evropsko vesoljsko agencijo Slovenija je od 1. januarja 2025 polnopravna članica Evropske vesoljske agencije (angleško The European Space Agency – Esa), kar slovenskim podjetjem in razvojnoraziskovalnim institucijam omogoča sodelovanje pri projektih Ese na področju vesoljskih tehnologij. Sodelujejo lahko podjetja in institucije, ki se ukvarjajo z inovativnimi materiali, tehnologijami, procesi ali informacijskimi rešitvami. Priložnost za sodelovanje z Eso imajo tudi mala in srednja podjetja, kar potrjujejo uspešni projekti podjetij iz Slovenije, ki so to priložnost že izkoristila. Slovenska vesoljska strategija 2030 Vlada je novembra 2023 sprejela prvo slovensko vesoljsko strategijo 2030 pod sloganom Majhni na Zemlji, veliki v vesolju , ki določa u
  https://www.gov.si/teme/sodelovanje-z-esa/

  Človek je prvič poslal sondo na komet - Žurnal24 Prijava Facebook prijava 1/2 EPA Sonda Philea je uspešno pristala na površino kometa 67P/Čurjumov-Gerasimenko. Velik korak za civilizacijo, je podvig komentiral generalni direktor Evropske vesoljske postaje. Gre za prvi pristanek na kometu v zgodovini človeštva in zgodovinski dosežek Evropske vesoljske agencije (ESA). Do pristanka je prišlo nekaj minut po peti popoldne po našem času. Po ločitvi Philae in Rosette so znanstveniki v Nemčiji prejeli signal z Rosette, ki jim je začela pošiljati podatke, in tudi s Philae. Philae so tako lahko sledili vso pot do površja kometa. Zgodovinski poskus pristanka na kometu s premerom več kot štiri kilometre se je odvijal več kot 500 milijonov kilometrov stran od Zemlje.   https://www.zurnal24.si/svet/clovek-je-prvic-poslal-sondo-na-komet-239670

Kuwait University has signed an agreement with the Kuwait Foundation for the Advancement of Sciences (KFAS) to establish and operate the Kuwait National Space Research Center. The signing took place in the presence of Kuwait University President, Prof. Dina Musaed Al-Mailem, and KFAS Director General, Dr. Ameenah Rajab Farhan, alongside senior officials from both institutions.   ### 1. Citation This Order may be cited as the Kenya Space Agency Order. ### 2. Interpretation In this Order—"Act" means the State Corporations Act (Cap. 466);"Agency" means the Kenya Space Agency established under paragraph 3;"Board" means the Board of the Agency appointed under paragraph 6;"Director-General" means the person appointed under paragraph 11;"Cabinet Secretary" means the Cabinet Secretary for the time being responsible for Defence;"Committee" means the State Corporations Advisory Committee;"member" means a person appointed to the Board under paragraph 6(1)(i). ## Part II – ESTABLISHMENT, POWERS AND FUNCTIONS OF KENYA SPACE AGENCY ### 3. Establishment of the Agency (1)There is hereby established a state corporation to be known as the Kenya Space Agency.(2)The Agency shall be a body corporate with perpetual succession and a common seal and shall, in its corporate name, be capable of—(a)suing and being sued;(b)taking, purchasing or otherwise acquiring, holding, charging or disposing of movable and immovable property;(c)receiving, investing, borrowing and lending money; and(d)doing or performing any such other thing or act, including entering into such contracts as may be
  https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/ln/2017/22

O Gabinete de Gestão do Programa Espacial Angolano (GGPEN) e a Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos (UAE Space Agency) assinaram, esta quinta-feira, 25 de Setembro, em Abidjan, Côte d’Ivoire, uma Carta de Intenção que estabelece as bases para o reforço da cooperação entre Angola e os Emirados Árabes Unidos no domínio espacial. - undated — MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate
  MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate Sep 06, 2026 Destaques O Space Enabled Research Group, integrado no MIT Media Lab do Massachusetts Institute of Technology (MIT), estendeu a afiliação do angolano Dr. Zolana João como Research Affiliate (Investigador Afiliado), vínculo que mantém com o grupo desde 2023. A decisão dá continuidade a uma colaboração científica desenvolvida ao longo dos últimos anos com uma das instituições académicas de maior referência mundial nas áreas de ciência, tecnologia e engenharia. A extensão da afiliação reforça a presença de Angola em redes internacionais de investigação e inovação e reconhece a contribuição de investigadores angolanos para a produção de conhecimento sobre a utilização responsável, inclusiva e sustentável das tecno
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/mit-space-enabled-estende-afiliacao-do-angolano-zolana-joao-como-research-affiliate
- undated — Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space
  Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Jul 23, 2026 Destaques Foi lançado, no dia 13 de Julho de 2026, o projecto Conecta Angola TVWS, uma iniciativa do MINTTICS, através do GGPEN e parceiros, que combina as capacidades do satélite ANGOSAT-2 com a tecnologia TV White Space (TVWS) para levar internet de banda larga a comunidades com acesso limitado à conectividade. A solução utiliza frequências não ocupadas do espectro televisivo para fornecer internet de forma eficiente, segura e com alcance de até 10 quilómetros, permitindo ampliar a cobertura digital e criar novas oportunidades de acesso a plataformas educativas, conteúdos digitais e serviços tecnológicos. Ecouter l'article Entre lutte contre la désinformation, régularisation des médias numériques, exécution des décisions de suspension et répression des publicités mensongères, la Haute Autorité de la Communication (HAC) entend renforcer son contrôle sur l&rsquo;espace médiatique gabonais. Dans son communiqué du 11 novembre 2026, l&rsquo;institution présidée par Germain Ngoyo Moussavou rappelle l&rsquo;étendue des obligations imposées aux professionnels de la communication et aux internautes. - undated — Training – A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System
  NEREUS Training &#8211; A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System &#8211; Mauritius Research and Innovation Council Skip to content Menu This training is the third and last of a series of courses within the Capacity Building programme for the NEREUS project funded by the United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) Ocean Innovation Challenge ( https://oceaninnovationchallenge.org ). The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Analysing these anomalies is very important in ensuring maritime security and environmental protection.   https://spacemauritius.com/nereus-training-a-space-based-maritime-surveillance-system/

  About ZINGSA &#8212; Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency Phone +263 8677009885 Email publicrelations@zingsa.ac.zw Hours Mon&#8211;Fri, 8am&#8211;4pm Contact Us About The ZINGSA story Promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial technologies for Zimbabwe&#8217;s development. Overview Who we are The Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency (ZINGSA) is a wholly government-owned entity established in terms of the Research Act [Chapter 10:22] and officially launched by President Emmerson Mnangagwa in 2021. ZINGSA is responsible for promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial and space technologies to support Zimbabwe&#8217;s socio-economic development and industrialization. - undated — 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について New!
  国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ
  https://www8.cao.go.jp/space/kokusai/20260901/260901.html

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De

Home / Hosting of 20 th International Committee on GNSS (ICG-20), Goa, India. November 13, 2026 International Committee on Global Navigation Satellite Systems (ICG), established in 2005 under the umbrella of the United Nations is an important forum to promote voluntary cooperation on matters of mutual interest related to civil satellite-based positioning, navigation, timing, and value-added services. ISRO/DoS had the privilege to host the 20 th annual meeting of ICG (ICG-20) from October 25 to 30, 2026 along with the United Nations Office for Outer Space Affairs (UNOOSA) at Goa, India.   Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully Home / Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully November 12, 2026 The ground static test of the PSLV third stage (PS3) was carried out successfully at the Static Test Facility in Satish Dhawan Space Centre, Sriharikota on November 12, 2026. Subsequent to the anomalies observed in the third stage of PSLV during the PSLV-C61/EOS-09 & PSLV-C62 / EOS-N1 missions, the configuration of the PS3 stage was revisited comprehensively w.r.t the design margins, raw material acceptance criteria, assembly procedures, and the quality checks that were followed so far.   ส่องสตาร์ตอัปด้าน “ธุรกิจอวกาศ” ของไทย ไม่แพ้ชาติใดในโลก Login หน้าแรก Tech Innovation ส่องสตาร์ตอัปด้าน “ธุรกิจอวกาศ” ของไทย ไม่แพ้ชาติใดในโลก By กัญญาภัค ทิศศรี By กัญญาภัค ทิศศรี NIA ดันสตาร์ตอัปสายอวกาศ ผ่าน “Space Economy: Lifting Off 2022” พร้อมอวดโฉมน้องใหม่ของวงการอวกาศ ที่มาพร้อมโซลูชั่นรุกไทยให้เป็นผู้ผลิตเทคโนฯ ลดการพึ่งพาจากต่างชาติ ก้าวสู่ ยุคเทคโนโลยีอวกาศ กับธุรกิจแห่งอนาคต มาทำความรู้จักกับคำว่า &ldquo; เทคโนโลยีอวกาศ &rdquo; หรือที่เราคุ้นหูกันบ่อย ๆ ก็คือ &ldquo; Space Tech &rdquo; แท้จริงแล้วมันคืออะไร แล้วจะนำไปต่อยอดในธุรกิจได้อย่างไร&nbsp; มาค้นหาคำตอบไปพร้อมกันจากโครงการ &ldquo; Space Economy : Lifting Off 2022 &rdquo; หนึ่งในกิจกรรมจากสำนักงานนวัตกรรมแห่งชาติ ( NIA ) ที่ดำเนินการร่วมกับภาคีความร่วมมืออวกาศไทยเพื่อผลักดันเศรษฐกิจอวกาศให้เกิดขึ้น ด้วยการพัฒนาผู้ประกอบการด้านเทคโนโลยี อวกาศ ให้มีบทบาทสามารถเติบโตได้ในอุตสาหกรรมอวกาศ และลดการนำเข้าเทคโนโลยีจากต่างประเ

- undated — โอกาสอนาคตเศรษฐกิจอวกาศ | ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล
  โอกาสอนาคตเศรษฐกิจอวกาศ | ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล Login หน้าแรก Tech Innovation โอกาสอนาคตเศรษฐกิจอวกาศ | ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล By ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล | สถาบันอนาคตไทยศึกษา By ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล | สถาบันอนาคตไทยศึกษา วิสัยทัศน์อนาคตด้านอวกาศ ครอบคลุมทั้งการปกป้องโลกให้ดีขึ้น การนำประโยชน์จากอวกาศมาใช้งานในโลก รวมทั้งการแสวงหาความเป็นไปได้อื่นๆ ที่มนุษย์ยังไปไม่ถึง ทิศทางอนาคต จากผู้นำเอกชนระดับโลกด้านอวกาศอย่าง &ldquo; อีลอน มัสก์ &rdquo; ผู้ก่อตั้ง บริษัทสเปซเอ็กซ์ (SpaceX) วางวิสัยทัศน์ไปสู่การตั้งถิ่นฐานของมนุษย์โลกที่ดาวอังคาร ในขณะที่ผู้นำภาคธุรกิจอย่าง &ldquo; เจฟฟ์ เบซอส &rdquo; จาก บริษัทบลู ออริจิน (Blue Origin) มีแผนอนาคตที่จะทำให้ดวงจันทร์เป็นอาณานิคมแห่งใหม่ของโลก เพื่อตอบโจทย์ข้อท้าทายที่โลกไม่สามารถดำเนินการได้ จากวิสัยทัศน์อนาคตของผู้นำระดับโลกเหล่านี้ เป็นกุญแจสำคัญในการต่อยอดโอกาสของ เศรษฐกิจอวกาศที่นานาประเทศจับตามอง 5 แนวทาง ได้แก่ 1. การเดินทางสู่อวกาศ (Going to space)

- undated — Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year
  Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year Business Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year WEDNESDAY, DECEMBER 07, 2022 สลับเป็นเสียงผู้ชาย Play Loading... Thailand is tapping Nasa expertise to set up its northern “space lab” in Chiang Rai province. A Nasa delegation has been invited to visit the Space Technology Laboratory at Wiangpapao Technical College on January 25 to exchange knowledge on the development of space affairs. The knowledge exchange was agreed at a videoconference meeting between Nasa officials and the House committee on communications, telecoms and digital economy and society,its vice chair Settapong Malisuwan said on Tuesday. Discussion during the meeting covered US-Thailand cooperation on space technology, climate change, global warming and natural crises, Settapong said. - undated — Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’
  Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’ In Focus Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’ MONDAY, SEPTEMBER 05, 2022 | THE NATION สลับเป็นเสียงผู้ชาย Play Loading... Thailand’s space agency will support the Royal Thai Armed Forces under a technology-sharing agreement announced on Monday. Under the agreement, the Geo-Informatics and Space Technology Development Agency (GISTDA) will share surveillance and other data to enhance national security and support missions by the military, GISTDA spokesman Siam Lawawirojwong said. The space agency will also boost skills among military personnel to protect Thailand&rsquo;s interests over land, sea, air and space, he added. Haji Azlikamil Napiah merasmikan Sambutan Hari Keluarga Peringkat MYSA 2026 bertempat di Dewan Seminar, Ibu Pejabat MYSA pada 16 November 2026. Sambutan ini bertepatan pemilihan November sebagai Bulan Keluarga Kebangsaan sejak tahun 2024. . Program merupakan anjuran bersama PUSPANITA MYSA dan Kelab MYSAC dengan tema sambutan “KASIH Keluarga, Jiwa Negara” telah disertai lebih 100 warga kerja. Secara tidak langsung, program mengeratkan hubungan di antara majikan dan pekerja selain meningkatkan amalan nilai murni melalui aktiviti sambutan hari keluarga. Yutu-2, or Jade Rabbit-2, captured an obscure but intriguing image about 80 meters from its location during the mission's 36th lunar day. - Fri, 20 Nov 2026 12:07:02 GMT — University of Seoul student wins agriculture category of World Bank Group AI hackathon
  Moon Tae-yoon, a student in the Department of International Relations at the University of Seoul, has won first place in the agriculture track of the Small AI for Development Hackathon Challenge hosted by the World Bank Group (WBG). Held from Oct. 3-4, the hackathon was designed to identify practical artificial intelligence (AI) solutions that can be deployed even in environments with limited internet access and electricity infrastructure. Competing as an individual, Moon presented “Dure,” an AI solution he independently conceptualized and developed.
